  Methodology: To address the limited availability of eye-tracking data for Chinese academic reading, we developed a lightweight webcam-based data collection platform using the open-source SearchGazer library and constructed the Chinese LIS Eye-Tracking Corpus (CLIS-ET). Three character-level eye-tracking features, first fixation duration (FFD), fixation number (FN), and total fixation duration (TFD), were incorporated into KPE models to evaluate their effects on extraction performance.
  Findings: Eye-tracking features consistently improved KPE performance. The combination of FN and TFD achieved the best results on the Att-BiLSTM+CRF model, indicating that readers' fixation behavior provides useful signals for identifying keyphrases in academic abstracts.
  Originality/value: This study introduces a cost-effective webcam-based eye-tracking approach for KPE and presents CLIS-ET, a Chinese academic eye-tracking corpus containing FFD, FN, and TFD features. The results demonstrate the value of incorporating human reading behavior into keyphrase extraction. Dataset and code: https://github.com/yan-xinyi/ET_AKE and https://github.com/yan-xinyi/Reading_ET_System.

</details>


### [147] [Can Released LLM Vocabularies Support Token-Level Estimation of Hidden Corpora?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10690)
*Qingjie Zhang,Xingzhang Ren,Zixuan Chen,Jinfeng Li,YueFeng Chen,Yitong Yang,Hui Xue,Dayiheng Liu,Han Qiu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Quantile-Guided Density Estimation (QGDE) 的方法，用于估计在隐藏预训练语料库上训练的语言模型的词元级语料组成比例。通过利用不同语料库训练的BPE分词器之间的稳定分布特性，QGDE实现了低误差的细粒度语料推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型的能力受其预训练语料组成的影响，但当模型权重发布时，原始训练语料往往不可见。现有方法只能推断整体混合比例或追踪特定词元组，缺乏对任意目标词元的精细估计能力。因此，需要一种能从发布的分词器词汇表中提取更细粒度语料信息的方法。

Method: 首先发现在不同语料库上训练的BPE分词器具有稳定的token ID与比例分布，进而提出Quantile-Guided Density Estimation (QGDE) 方法：利用已知语料库的分布趋势进行迁移，通过多个分位数趋势近似目标分布，并采用局部密度加权生成词元级别的估计结果。

Result: 在受控环境和使用公开SmolLM分词器的真实场景中，QGDE在词元级别估计的平均相对误差低至3.00%，聚合为类别级别混合比例后的误差为3.08%。

Conclusion: 发布的分词器词汇表可作为推断语言模型训练语料细粒度组成的有效信号，QGDE为揭示黑箱语料构成提供了一种高精度、细粒度的分析工具。

Abstract: Pretraining corpus composition shapes LLM capabilities, but it often remains hidden even when model weights are released. Prior work has inferred corpus mixtures or traced specific token groups from released tokenizer vocabularies; in contrast, we estimate corpus ratios for arbitrary target tokens. We first show that BPE tokenizers trained on different corpora share stable token ID--ratio distributions, motivating distribution transfer from known corpora to a target tokenizer trained on hidden corpora. We then propose Quantile-Guided Density Estimation (QGDE), which approximates this distribution with multiple quantile trends and uses local density weighting to produce token-level estimates. In controlled settings and a realistic setting using the released SmolLM tokenizer, QGDE achieves mean relative errors as low as 3.00% for token-level estimation and 3.08% after aggregation into category-level mixtures. These results suggest that released tokenizer vocabularies provide a useful signal for fine-grained corpus estimation beyond coarse composition inference.

</details>


### [148] [SPIEval: Evaluating Large Language Models as Mobile Assistants over Scattered Personal Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10692)
*Junjie Ye,Zhuohui Sheng,Shaofan Liu,Yulun Zhu,Wenjie Fu,Dingwei Zhu,Ming Zhang,Yujiong Shen,Weichao Wang,Xin Zhao,Shihan Dou,Tao Gui,Qi Zhang,Xuanjing Huang,Pluto Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SPIEval是一个基于人类整理的基准测试，用于评估大型语言模型在移动助手场景中利用跨应用个人信息完成用户指令的能力，涵盖五种认知能力，实验表明现有模型表现有限，主要问题在于信息定位不准确。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于缺乏专门的基准测试，大型语言模型在移动助手场景中利用分散的个人数据完成复杂任务的能力尚不清楚，因此需要构建一个真实、多维度的评测基准。

Method: 提出了SPIEval基准，包含250个任务、4335条个人记录，分布在10个应用中，支持21种工具和多轮交互；基于五种认知能力设计任务，并对9个代表性大模型进行系统评估。

Result: 最佳模型GPT-5.5 (xhigh) 仅达到57.3%的准确率，最弱模型为16.4%；79%的错误源于信息定位不准确，少于2%的检索使用高级搜索策略，模型间搜索效率差异显著。

Conclusion: 当前基于大语言模型的移动助手在处理跨应用个人数据时存在根本性局限，尤其在信息检索与验证方面，SPIEval为未来研究提供了可衡量的测试平台。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed as mobile assistants, where a key challenge is leveraging personal information scattered across multiple applications (apps) to complete user instructions. However, due to the lack of dedicated benchmarks, their capabilities remain poorly understood. To address this gap, we introduce SPIEval, a human-curated benchmark grounded in five cognitive capabilities (i.e., reasoning, disambiguation, integration, preference inference, and multi-intent decomposition). SPIEval comprises 250 tasks spanning 4,335 personal records distributed across 10 apps and supports multi-turn interaction through 21 tools. Analysis shows that the benchmark exhibits diverse scenarios, challenging tasks, scattered information, controllable environments, and verifiable outcomes. We evaluate nine representative LLMs and find substantial room for improvement. The best-performing model, GPT-5.5 (xhigh), achieves only 57.3% accuracy, while the weakest achieves just 16.4%. Further analysis reveals that 79% of failures stem from inaccurate information localization, as LLMs often commit to plausible but incorrect information instead of continuing retrieval for verification. We also find that fewer than 2% of retrieval actions employ advanced search methods and observe substantial variation in search efficiency across models. These findings expose fundamental limitations of current LLM-based mobile assistants and motivate future research in this direction. Data and code are available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/Junjie-Ye/SPIEval.

</details>


### [149] [EVIL-Detect for NLPCC 2026 Shared Task 6: LLM-Generated Text Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10698)
*Hongrui Bao,Hangyu Rong,Zhuoshang Wang,Yubing Ren,Yanan Cao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为EVIL-Detect的多信号集成框架，用于检测中文场景下的大语言模型生成文本，在NLPCC 2026共享任务中排名第一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型的发展，亟需可靠的方法来区分人类撰写、模型生成及模型润色的中文文本。

Method: 结合编辑程度回归、零样本似然对比信号、词汇统计和保守文本规则，并采用冲突感知融合与校准决策边界。

Result: 在官方评测中达到0.8888的macro-F1分数，排名第一。

Conclusion: EVIL-Detect在分布外场景下表现出强鲁棒性，有效提升了LLM生成文本的检测能力。

Abstract: The rapid development of large language models (LLMs) has increased the need for reliable detection of LLM-generated text, especially in realistic Chinese scenarios involving human-written text (HWT), LLM-generated text (LGT), and LLM-refined text (HLT). This paper presents EVIL-Detect, a multi-signal ensemble framework with conflict-aware fusion for NLPCC 2026 Shared Task 6. The system integrates edit-extent regression, zero-shot likelihood-contrast signals, lexical statistics, and conservative text rules. With calibrated decision boundaries and conflict-aware integration, our system improves robustness under strong out-of-distribution shifts, achieving a macro-F1 score of 0.8888 and ranking first in the official evaluation. Our code is available at https://github.com/bbbbhrrrr/evildetect.

</details>


### [150] [Most biomedical publications show signs of LLM-assisted writing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10715)
*Lena Holzwarth,Rita González-Márquez,Dmitry Kobak*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种基于词汇频率变化的无偏方法，估计生物医学论文中大语言模型（LLM）使用的普遍性，发现到2025年底89%的论文显示出LLM相关词汇增加。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM在学术写作中的广泛应用，存在学术不端和欺诈的风险，亟需可靠的方法来监测学术出版物中LLM修改文本的流行程度。

Method: 提出并验证了一种基于词汇频率变化的新无偏估计方法，分析PubMed Central中开放获取生物医学论文全文中的LLM相关词汇使用情况。

Result: 到2025年底，89%的论文显示出LLM相关词汇的过度使用；讨论部分使用LLM的比例为68%，方法部分为32%，但方法部分的整体使用率也超过50%。

Conclusion: 该方法能提供可靠的LLM使用估计，对制定未来学术写作政策和指南具有重要意义。

Abstract: Over the past several years, LLM-powered chatbots and agents have become widely used as a tool for academic writing. LLM-assisted writing can be valuable by removing language barriers but at the same time causes concerns about misconduct and fraud. To inform policy decisions, it is necessary to monitor the prevalence of LLM-altered texts in scholarly publications. Despite some recent progress in this direction, no existing method can produce reliable estimates. Here we suggest and validate a new unbiased approach to estimate LLM usage in a corpus of texts based on changing word frequencies. We apply our method to the full texts of open-access biomedical papers from Pubmed Central, and show that by the end of 2025, 89% of papers show excess of LLM-associated vocabulary. We also find that LLMs are twice as likely to be used when writing a paragraph in the Discussion section (68%) compared to a paragraph in the Methods section (32%), but even inside the Methods section, the overall prevalence of LLM usage is over 50%. We believe that our estimates are crucial to shape future guidelines and policies.

</details>


### [151] [Mitigating Context Interference for Reliable and Efficient Search Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10743)
*Boyang Xue,Bin Wu,Shuofei Qiao,Sheng Wang,Rui Wang,Yiming Du,Hongru Wang,Jeff Z. Pan,Emine Yilmaz,Kam-Fai Wong,Aldo Lipani*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究系统探讨了多轮搜索代理中的上下文干扰问题，发现最新检索到的文档是主要干扰源，并提出基于蒸馏的上下文优化器来动态缓解该问题，结合强化学习训练显著提升了代理的可靠性与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多轮搜索代理在处理复杂任务时会产生长而复杂的上下文，其中包含大量无关信息，导致上下文干扰，影响模型的可靠性和效率，因此需要系统研究其成因并提出优化方法。

Method: 通过系统分析不同上下文部分对干扰的影响，识别主要干扰源；设计基于蒸馏的上下文优化器以动态精炼上下文；并将上下文优化机制融入强化学习训练流程中进行验证。

Result: 发现最新检索到的文档是上下文干扰的主要来源；所提出的上下文优化器能有效减少干扰；结合强化学习训练后，搜索代理的性能、可靠性和效率均显著提升。

Conclusion: 缓解上下文干扰对提升多轮搜索代理性能至关重要，'先优化上下文再生成'是一种具有潜力的新范式，为AI代理的设计提供了新方向。

Abstract: Recent research empowers Large Language Models (LLMs) as multi-turn search agents to iteratively retrieve and generate outputs until complex tasks are solved. However, the contexts of multi-turn search agents are lengthy and complex. For example, the retrieved set of documents in each turn would inevitably introduce irrelevant information that distracts LLMs, referring to \textit{context interference}, potentially hindering the reliability and efficiency of search agents. Therefore, we conduct a systematic study on context interference in multi-turn search agents, focusing on investigating i) which parts of the context of search agents will contribute to the context interference, ii) how to refine the contexts of search agents to mitigate the interference, and iii) can incorporating context refinement into search agent training yield further improvements. We reveal that interference primarily arises from the latest retrieved documents. Based on the explored findings, we then introduce a distill-based context refiner to dynamically mitigate context interference for multi-turn search agents. Finally, we validate that incorporating context refinement into RL training pipelines of search agents can significantly enhance both reliability and efficiency. This study highlights the importance of mitigating context interference of search agents, inspiring a novel paradigm of ``refine context and then generate'' for AI agents.

</details>


### [152] [Assessing Reliability of BERT-Based Models on Question Answering Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10806)
*Pooja Yadav,Priyanka Harjule,Basant Agarwal,Marko Robnik Šikonja*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究评估了基于BERT的问答模型（如RoBERTa、ALBERT、DistilBERT）在蒙特卡洛Dropout和输入改写下的响应稳定性，发现RoBERTa可靠性更高，而ALBERT和DistilBERT存在显著不一致性，强调在实际应用中需同时评估准确性和稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管基于Transformer的问答模型在准确率上表现优异，但其可靠性（如预测稳定性）尚未被充分探索，限制了其在现实场景中的可信部署。

Method: 采用蒙特卡洛Dropout（MCD）评估模型内部变异性，并通过输入改写测试外部扰动下的稳定性；在SQuAD和QuAC数据集上分析四种BERT类模型的预测一致性。

Result: RoBERTa表现出更高的可靠性，而ALBERT和DistilBERT在不同dropout率和输入扰动下出现显著不一致；统计结果表明MCD不会破坏推理动态，适合作为可靠性度量工具。

Conclusion: 在评估问答模型时，除准确率外，还应重视稳定性；RoBERTa更适合对可靠性要求高的应用场景，而轻量模型需进一步优化其预测一致性。

Abstract: Reliability estimation of large language models is in many cases as crucial as their accuracy, as reliable models are more trustworthy, robust, and suitable for practical applications. Recent advancements in natural language processing (NLP), particularly those based on transformer architectures, have significantly accelerated progress across various NLP tasks. This study focuses on the reliability of transformer-based question answering (QA) models, specifically BERT models and its variants (RoBERTa, ALBERT, DistilBERT). These encoder-only pretrained transformers have demonstrated remarkable accuracy in QA tasks that can be treated as classification tasks. However, their reliability remains underexplored. This study evaluates the reliability of four BERT-based models by assessing response stability under two conditions: (1) internal model variations induced via Monte Carlo Dropout (MCD) and (2) input perturbations through paraphrasing. Using the SQuAD and QuAC datasets, we investigate how dropout rates affect prediction consistency and whether lexical changes impact answer stability. Our findings reveal that RoBERTa maintains higher reliability, whereas AlBERT and DistilBERT exhibit significant inconsistencies. Statistical analyses confirm that enabling MCD during prediction does not disrupt inference dynamics, validating its effectiveness as a reliability metric. These findings underscore the importance of evaluating both accuracy and stability in QA models to ensure stability in real-world applications.

</details>


### [153] [Surfacing the Unsaid: CUE-Bench for Affective Stance in Chinese Discourse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10810)
*Zhenyan Zheng,Yunyao Zhang,Junxi Sheng,Junqing Yu,Zikai Song*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了CUE Bench，一个聚焦于情感立场的中文未言明情感基准，通过引入显性与隐性情感、语用意图和细粒度情绪之间的结构化关系，提升模型对深层情感理解的能力。实验表明，该方法显著提高了细粒度情绪识别和语用意图检测的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有情感分析基准主要关注表面极性或最终情绪类别，缺乏对显性表达、隐性情感、语用意图和细粒度情绪之间交互的结构化建模，导致模型难以捕捉隐含、弱化、反转或语用重塑的情感含义。

Method: 提出CUE Bench基准，构建了九种人类可解释的情感立场，涵盖显性与隐性情感的极性互动，并提供语用意图和细粒度情绪标注，支持结构化的情感推理。

Result: 实验结果显示，引入情感立场使细粒度情绪识别准确率提升了3.5个百分点，语用意图检测提升了7.8个百分点，优于强基线模型。

Conclusion: CUE Bench通过结构化的情感立场建模，有效提升了对话中深层情感理解的能力，为情感分析提供了更精细的评估框架。

Abstract: Emotion understanding in discourse requires reasoning beyond surface sentiment because speakers often convey affect through indirect, implicit, polite, ironic, or deliberately mismatched expressions. Existing emotion benchmarks mainly annotate surface polarity or final emotion categories, while lacking a structured account of how explicit expression, implicit affect, pragmatic intent, and fine grained emotion interact. This limitation makes current evaluations insensitive to cases where affective meaning is concealed, weakened, inverted, or pragmatically reshaped, thereby obscuring model failures in deeper emotion understanding. To address this gap, we introduce CUE Bench, a Chinese Unsaid Emotion benchmark that centers on Affective Stance and covers diverse communicative scenarios. CUE Bench constructs nine human interpretable affective stances from explicit implicit polarity interaction and further provides intent and fine grained emotion annotations for structured affective inference. Experiments show that incorporating Affective Stance improves fine grained emotion recognition by 3.5 percentage points and pragmatic intent detection by 7.8 percentage points over strong baselines.

</details>


### [154] [Reference-Free Post-Training of Open Large Language Models for Multilingual Machine Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10812)
*Chris Han,Pengzhi Gao,Pei Fu,Jian Luan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种无需参考译文的多语言机器翻译后训练方法，基于监督微调模型结合组相对策略优化（GRPO）与奖励机制，并通过线性插值提升翻译质量，在46种语言上均超越现有开源及专有系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决多语言机器翻译中缺乏高质量参考译文进行后训练的问题，探索在无参考情况下如何有效利用大语言模型提升翻译性能。

Method: 从监督微调的MiLMMT-46-v0.1模型出发，采用组相对策略优化（GRPO），结合两种无参考质量估计模型的平均奖励，并通过语言识别门控机制调节奖励；随后对监督微调（SFT）与强化学习（RL）模型检查点进行线性插值，得到最终模型MiLMMT-46-v1.0；同时研究了在线策略蒸馏的效果。

Result: 所提方法在46种语言上 consistently 提升翻译质量，优于多个强开源基线（如Seed-X、HY-MT2、TranslateGemma），并在无参考评估指标上超过Google Translate、Gemini 3 Pro和GPT-5等专有系统；在线策略蒸馏能达到但未超越RL加插值的质量水平。

Conclusion: 结合GRPO与检查点插值是一种有效的无参考后训练方法，显著提升多语言翻译性能，且优于多种先进开源与闭源系统，推动了开放大模型在多语言翻译中的应用。

Abstract: We study reference-free post-training for multilingual machine translation with open large language models. Starting from the supervised-finetuned MiLMMT-46-v0.1 models, we apply Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) with a reward that averages two reference-free quality estimation models and is gated by language identification. We then linearly interpolate the supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and reinforcement learning (RL) model checkpoints to obtain MiLMMT-46-v1.0. Across 46 languages, the resulting models consistently improve translation quality over their SFT counterparts, outperform strong recent open baselines, including Seed-X, HY-MT2, and TranslateGemma, and achieve leading reference-free scores against evaluated proprietary systems such as Google Translate, Gemini 3 Pro, and GPT-5. We further investigate on-policy distillation and find that it reaches, but does not surpass, the quality frontier achieved by RL with checkpoint interpolation. We release the models and code to facilitate future research.

</details>


### [155] [VibeLifeBench: Can Your Life Agent Be Proactive and Persistent in a Living World?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10875)
*Xiaohongshu Inc*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了VibeLifeBench，一个包含200个长周期任务的基准测试，用于评估大语言模型代理在模拟现实生活中多周、动态变化环境中的持续性与主动性表现，结果显示当前前沿模型在真实生活辅助方面仍有较大差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大语言模型代理评估主要基于短时间、静态环境下的任务，无法反映真实生活中的长期、动态和隐含约束场景，因此需要一种新的评估方式来衡量代理在复杂现实环境中的持续表现能力。

Method: 设计了VibeLifeBench基准，包含十个日常生活领域的200个长周期任务，每个任务在一个具有22个模拟服务的动态模拟世界中运行数周，世界自主推进且多数变化无提示；通过细粒度、加权的评估机制，仅根据代理留下的行为痕迹（如最终状态、行动时机、对隐含约束的遵守）进行评分。

Result: 对七个前沿大语言模型进行了测试，所有模型得分均较低，表明当前代理在长期一致性、主动感知环境变化和处理未明说约束方面表现不足。

Conclusion: 当前的大语言模型代理距离胜任真实生活中的长期辅助任务仍有显著差距，VibeLifeBench为未来研究提供了可开源的测试平台和评估框架。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are increasingly deployed as personal assistants. Existing evaluations, however, mostly use short, self-contained requests in static environments. Everyday life assistance is different. A task runs for weeks rather than minutes. The world keeps changing while the agent is not being prompted. Many constraints are never stated outright. An agent that merely answers the request in front of it will fail at such a task. What is needed instead is an agent that stays proactive and consistent. It decides on its own when to act, when to ask, and when to stay silent. It notices changes that nobody announced. It keeps one plan coherent from the first day to the last. No current benchmark measures this. We introduce VibeLifeBench, a benchmark of 200 long-horizon tasks across ten everyday-life domains. Each task is a scripted multi-week timeline in a simulated world of 22 mock services. The world advances on its own clock, and many of its changes are silent, so only an agent that re-inspects the world discovers them. Every task is graded by fine-grained, weighted checks that read only what the agent actually left behind, covering the end state, the timeliness of its actions, and whether it upheld the implicit constraints. We evaluate seven frontier models. All of them score low, which shows how far current agents are from assisting with real life. We will open-source all tasks, environments, and the evaluation framework.

</details>


### [156] [X2-Turn: Frame-Synchronous Dual-Head Modeling for Joint Streaming ASR and Turn State Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10878)
*Kaiqi Fu,Rime Wen,Altman Lin,Shawn Qin,Roy Gan,Hao Wang,Qian Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出X2-Turn方法，基于预训练Voxtral实时模型，实现帧同步的细粒度转录状态预测，兼顾高准确率与低延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模块化方法在语音对话系统中对用户打断、反馈语和话语结束的实时区分存在不连续性和依赖辅助ASR模型的问题，导致响应性差和系统复杂度高。

Method: 基于预训练的Voxtral Realtime模型，引入帧同步的转录状态头，与ASR头共享流式表示并行运行，实现帧级别的ASR词元和转录状态联合预测。

Result: 在中英双语Easy-Turn测试集上验证了X2-Turn的有效性，实现了准确的转录检测同时保持低延迟。

Conclusion: X2-Turn通过延迟流建模实现了高效、低延迟的帧同步转录状态预测，优于传统模块化方法，适用于实际口语对话系统。

Abstract: Accurate and responsive turn-taking is essential for spoken dialogue systems, which must distinguish in real time between user interruptions, backchannels that should be ignored, and the completion of an utterance. Prior modular approaches typically optimize turn state prediction at the utterance or fixed-chunk level, creating a mismatch with the continuous turn state estimate, and often depend on an auxiliary ASR model, which limits responsiveness and increases overall system complexity. Therefore, we present X2-Turn, a frame-synchronous turn state prediction method via delayed-stream modeling. Specifically, building on the pretrained Voxtral Realtime model, we introduce a frame-synchronous turn state head that operates in parallel with the ASR head on shared streaming representations, jointly predicting ASR tokens and fine-grained turn states at the frame level. We evaluate our method on the bilingual Chinese-English Easy-Turn test sets, and the results demonstrate its effectiveness in achieving accurate turn-taking detection while maintaining low latency.

</details>


### [157] [Certify or Refuse: A Cross-Model Map for Selective Risk Control with Coverage Floors under Covariate Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10893)
*Jiamiao Liu,Dewen Qiao,Yu Zhang,Xuetao Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了在有界比率协变量漂移下认证选择性预测器的可行性边界和复杂性映射，提出了风险与自动化底限的双资源复杂性模型，并通过理论与实证验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了在分布偏移场景下确保选择性预测器的可靠性和自动化响应底限，需建立兼顾选择条件风险和目标域覆盖率的认证机制。

Method: 提出Floor Certification Map框架，结合标记源数据中的风险和未标记目标样本中的底限，分析局部可行性边界，并设计基于预注册分层漂移模型的可实现上界方法。

Result: 证明了双资源复杂性地图的存在性，给出了模型间的匹配上下界（Model-B下界与Model-A上界），并展示了在SQuAD-to-NewsQA等任务中无违规记录的实证结果。

Conclusion: 认证选择性预测在有界比率协变量漂移下具有可量化的复杂性结构，自动化底限是构建该结构的关键因素，且预注册模型支持可行的实施路径。

Abstract: Certified selective predictors attain whatever coverage they attain; operators impose an automation floor: answer at least a $β$-fraction of shifted target traffic with at most an $α$-fraction of answers wrong. Under bounded-ratio covariate shift we prove the Floor Certification Map: once that floor must be certified alongside the selection-conditioned risk $α$, certification acquires a feasibility frontier and a two-resource complexity map, additive up to constants: risk in labeled source, the floor in unlabeled target samples. The rates are local, needing a regular frontier margin, slack below the local-regime threshold, and lattice conditions: pre-registered with a lattice margin for the upper bounds, compatible per-slack for the lower. The displayed split is the operational route; oracle weights also allow a labeled-source floor estimate. Three model-tagged results: a lower bound (Model-B), a matching oracle-weight upper bound (Model-A), and an implementable upper bound (Model-B') valid under a pre-registered exact stratified-shift model with nuisance cost priced explicitly. The match is across these models rather than a single-model minimax theorem, and necessarily so: over the full bounded-ratio class no unknown-weight procedure matches at any sample size (Model-B is inconsistent, witnessed at $α=β=1/2$). The nuisance's necessity is only partially settled. Complexity tracks a localized accepted-region functional, not global effective sample size (ESS), on both sides, though a fixed-ESS separation theorem is left open; both lower-bound axes vanish as $β\to0$, so the floor creates the map. Empirically, the registered bite family diverges with log-log slope $-2.002$ within its pre-registered band; a 1,024-cell audit records 0 violations where the formal certificates fire; and a single-corpus SQuAD-to-NewsQA feasibility audit returns honest refusal.

</details>


### [158] [FaithformBench: Benchmarking Faithfulness of Mathematical Chain-of-Thought Autoformalisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10916)
*Rob Cornish,Iacopo Ghinassi,Po-Hung Yeh,Shuqi Liu,Qiyuan Xu,Haoxuan Yin,Dominik Wagner,Wenda Li,Yee Whye Teh,Luke Ong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种新的自动形式化（AF）系统保真度评估基准，通过自动生成无效的扰动推理步骤，评估系统在保持有效和无效性方面的表现，发现当前AF系统普遍存在“谄媚”现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AF系统保真度评估方法依赖昂贵的人工标注或不可靠的LLM判断，且仅关注正确输入，无法评估对错误输入的处理。因此需要一种低成本、可扩展且能同时评估正负样本的方法。

Method: 通过自动构造无效的扰动推理步骤，构建包含正例（未扰动）和反例（扰动）的数据集，衡量AF系统在保持原始步骤有效性的同时是否也保持扰动步骤的无效性。

Result: 在四个数学数据集上的八个AF系统中应用该方法，发现多数系统会“静默纠正”无效输入为可证明语句，表现出明显的sycophancy；最能保持有效性的微调系统反而最严重地破坏了无效性。

Conclusion: 当前AF系统在有效性保持与无效性保持之间存在张力，过度追求正确性可能导致对错误输入的不忠实转换，揭示了模型忠实在自动形式化中的核心挑战。

Abstract: Autoformalisation (AF) systems map natural language reasoning steps into formal statements in a proof assistant such as Lean. We consider how to assess the faithfulness of these systems. Existing approaches require expensive human-annotated ground truth, or rely on LLM judges or embedding models, which come with limited guarantees of accuracy. In addition, these methods typically only consider inputs that are known to be correct, and therefore do not assess whether the AF translates incorrect inputs faithfully. To address these limitations, we propose a new benchmark for AF faithfulness that is cheap to apply, sound under weak assumptions, and assesses both positive and negative examples. Our method is based on automatically generating perturbed reasoning steps that are designed to be invalid, and then measuring validity preservation on unperturbed steps and invalidity preservation on perturbed steps. We apply our method to eight AF systems across four mathematical datasets, and observe pervasive sycophancy: many AFs "silently correct" invalid inputs into provable statements. The most validity-preserving fine-tuned AFs are also the most sycophantic, suggesting a tension between validity and invalidity preservation in current AF systems.

</details>


### [159] [A Cost-Efficient Routing Pipeline for Multilingual Short-Text Classification Using Small Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10939)
*Wajdi Ben Saad,Safa Madiouni*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多语言短文本分类中的选择性翻译路由策略，通过将低资源语言翻译为英语后再进行零样本分类，提升了低资源语言的分类性能，且该方法无需任务特定的微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多语言短文本分类系统在统一推理策略下往往掩盖了高资源与低资源语言之间的性能差异，导致低资源语言表现较差。本文旨在通过差异化处理提升整体分类效果，尤其是改善低资源语言的表现。

Method: 采用预训练的紧凑型句子编码器，构建完全自托管的流水线，对不同语言按资源等级划分，仅对低资源或中低资源语言进行翻译至英语后再进行零样本分类，比较不同路由策略（如仅翻译低资源、全部翻译等）的效果。

Result: 在SIB-200和MASSIVE两个基准上测试表明：选择性翻译显著提升低资源语言的Macro-F1分数（如SIB-200中从0.4632升至0.6828），但在不同任务中最优路由策略不同（SIB-200最优为仅翻译低资源，MASSIVE最优为全部翻译）。同时报告了各层级的质量增益与延迟。

Conclusion: 选择性翻译是改善低资源语言分类性能的有效手段，但最佳路由策略依赖于具体任务；应基于语言层级评估性能增益与延迟，而非单一全局指标。

Abstract: Multilingual short-text classification supports operational systems such as content moderation, customer support routing, and intent recognition, yet aggregate evaluation often hides large differences between high-resource and low-resource languages. Uniform inference policies are simple to deploy, but they assume that all languages are equally well served. In this work, we evaluate a fixed-list routing strategy that keeps stronger languages on a direct multilingual path and selectively sends weaker languages through translation into English before zero-shot classification. The pipeline is fully self-hosted, uses pretrained compact sentence encoders, and requires no task-specific fine-tuning.
  We test the approach on two benchmarks chosen to differ in scale and label granularity: a 15-language subset of SIB-200 for seven-way topic classification and a 15-locale subset of MASSIVE for intent classification over an official 60-intent inventory. On SIB-200, the best overall configuration is R1, which translates only the low-resource tier: high-tier and mid-tier Macro-F1 remain unchanged, while low-tier Macro-F1 rises from 0.4632 to 0.6828. On the MASSIVE subset, the same low-tier intervention raises low-tier Macro-F1 from 0.2143 to 0.4417, but the best overall result is obtained by full translation, R3, at Macro-F1 0.4647. Across these two benchmarks, selective translation is a reliable intervention for weaker languages, whereas the optimal routing boundary depends on the task. We therefore report routing through tier-level quality gains and tier-level latency rather than a single global efficiency score.

</details>


### [160] [REAP: Relation-Aware Elicitation and Parsing for Closed-Book Knowledge Base Construction from LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10963)
*Thanh-Dan Bui,Thanh-Trung Do,Tuan-Phong Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: REAP系统在AKBC 2026共享任务中，基于Mistral-Small-24B-Instruct-2501模型，采用结构化思维链、关系特定查询策略和空集门机制，在无微调、32B参数限制下实现了0.62的macro-F1分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在封闭式设定下从语言模型中构建知识库，受限于模型规模和无微调条件，探索如何有效提取参数化知识。

Method: 结合结构化思维链推理、针对不同关系设计的查询策略，以及基于推理的空集过滤机制，直接将结果提取为有效的JSON数组。

Result: 在测试集上达到0.62的macro-F1分数，其中countryLandBordersCountry（F1=0.95）、companyTradesAtStockExchange（F1=0.73）和hasArea（F1=0.77）表现尤为突出。

Conclusion: REAP系统在严格约束条件下展示了高效的知识提取能力，验证了无需微调也能通过推理策略充分挖掘大模型中的隐含知识。

Abstract: We present the REAP system for the AKBC Shared Task 2026 on constructing knowledge bases from language models in a closed-book setting, subject to a budget of at most 32B parameters and no model fine-tuning. Our system combines structured chain-of-thought reasoning, relation-specific query strategies, and a reasoning-based empty-set gate to elicit parametric knowledge, followed by direct extraction into valid JSON arrays. On the test set, the system, built on the Mistral-Small-24B-Instruct-2501 model, achieves a macro-F1 score of 0.62, with particularly strong results on countryLandBordersCountry (F1 = 0.95), companyTradesAtStockExchange (F1 = 0.73), and hasArea (F1 = 0.77). Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/yammdd/AKBC-Shared-Task-2026.

</details>


### [161] [ReLTEx: Reliable LLM-based Taxonomy Expansion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10970)
*Zeinab Ghamlouch,Mehwish Alam*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为ReLTEx的可靠基于大语言模型（LLM）的分类法扩展框架，结合了候选生成、结构感知验证和递归扩展控制，以提高生成分类法的一致性和质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在生成语义相关概念方面表现出色，但直接使用其生成结果常导致噪声、冗余或层次不一致问题，限制了其在自动分类法扩展中的可靠性。

Method: ReLTEx框架结合LLM驱动的候选生成与结构感知验证，并采用递归扩展控制机制，在扩展过程中减少幻觉并提升层次一致性。

Result: 在基准分类法上的实验表明，ReLTEx在掩码式分类法扩展设置下，通过多种验证策略和改进的评估指标，显著提升了扩展结果的可靠性和语义连贯性，且得到了人工评估的支持。

Conclusion: ReLTEx能有效提升LLM生成分类法的可靠性和结构一致性，为自动化分类法扩展提供了更稳健的解决方案。

Abstract: Recent advances in Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated strong capabilities in generating semantically relevant concepts and relations, making them promising tools for taxonomy enrichment. However, directly relying on LLM-generated expansions often leads to noisy, redundant, or hierarchically inconsistent structures, limiting their reliability for automated taxonomy expansion. In this paper, we present ReLTEx, a framework for reliable LLM-based taxonomy expansion. ReLTEx combines LLM-driven candidate generation with structure-aware validation and recursive expansion control to improve the consistency and quality of generated taxonomies by reducing hallucinations. We evaluate the proposed framework using benchmark taxonomies under a masked taxonomy expansion setting and compare multiple validation strategies. Experimental results, supported by both adapted evaluation metrics and human evaluation, demonstrate that ReLTEx produces more reliable and semantically coherent taxonomy expansions.

</details>


### [162] [MUSE: A Full-Text Cross-Domain Knowledge Base of Scientific Problems, Solutions, and Rationales](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10974)
*Tsofia Cohen,Tom Hope*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了MUSE，一个包含科学问题-解决方案-理由（P-S-R）三元组的多领域全文资源，通过专家标注和自动化抽取构建了37K个高质量三元组，并发现基于理由监督的LLM在复杂问题上表现更好，但在简单问题上可能表现下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学论文中蕴含大量技术难题及其解决方法与选择依据，但缺乏系统化结构化表示，因此需要构建细粒度的问题-解法-理由知识库以支持科学问题求解的研究与应用。

Method: 设计了涵盖问题、解法、理由及它们之间关系的丰富标注模式，人工标注579段全文段落，并通过模块化信息抽取流水线扩展到37K个源文档对齐的P-S-R三元组，同时尝试用理由监督训练大语言模型进行科学问题求解。

Result: 成功构建了MUSE资源，包含专家标注数据和37K规模的P-S-R知识库；评估显示抽取组件性能良好；初步实验表明理由监督能提升模型在多约束复杂问题上的表现，但可能损害其在简单问题上的效果。

Conclusion: MUSE为科学问题求解提供了有价值的结构化资源，证明了显式建模推理依据的重要性，但也揭示了在不同复杂度任务中使用理由监督需权衡利弊。

Abstract: Scientific papers contain fine-grained records of problem solving: authors mention technical obstacles and methods that were used to address them, often along with reasoning on why those methods were chosen. We introduce MUSE (Mining Underlying Scientific Explanations), a full-text, multi-domain resource of scientific Problem-Solution-Rationale (P-S-R) triplets. We curate 579 expert-annotated full-text paragraphs, with a rich annotation schema covering salient problem, solution, and rationale spans, solves and rationale_of links and conceptual coreference. A modular extraction pipeline scales this annotation to build a high-quality knowledge base of 37K source-grounded P-S-R triplets. We evaluate the extraction components and include a preliminary experiment training a rationale-supervised LLM for scientific problem solving. Interestingly, we find that rationale supervision improves performance on complex, multi-constraint problems but can harm performance on simpler ones.

</details>


### [163] [What Iterated Self-Feeding Probes of Language Models Measure, and a test that separates the construction from the model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10986)
*Nicolás Vera Zúñiga*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了通过模型自身输出循环反馈来探测语言模型的方法，提出了一种新的耦合方式以精确测量损伤传播。研究发现该方法同时衡量了两种不同性质的量：一种由构造决定且模型不变，另一种则真实反映模型特性。作者通过固定构造变模型或固定模型变构造的测试区分二者，并验证了工具的有效性，纠正了此前误判的四个结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨基于自反馈的语言模型探测方法究竟测量的是什么，尤其是在使用环形token序列和Glauber动力学框架下，如何准确区分测量结果是源于探测构造本身还是模型的真实属性。

Method: 采用两个仅有一个token不同的环形序列，在共同随机数条件下进行同步演化，利用最大耦合分析损伤传播；通过固定构造变化模型或固定模型变化构造的方式，识别哪些测量值具有模型依赖性；并在Domany-Kinzel模型上复现结果以验证方法正确性。

Result: 发现损伤光锥和Lyapunov指数半径缩放等量是构造决定的、跨19个模型保持不变；而Lyapunov指数过零点和吸引子占比则能稳定反映模型差异；复现了Domany-Kinzel模型的损伤场并发现了四个因估计失败而需撤回的错误结论。

Conclusion: 当前这类自反馈探测方法可能混淆构造效应与模型真实特性，必须通过控制变量测试加以区分；本文提供的分离准则和开源工具包可帮助避免此类误判，提升对语言模型动态行为的理解可靠性。

Abstract: A growing class of methods probes a language model by feeding it its own output: self-consistency, iterated refinement, agentic loops. We ask what such a probe measures, in a construction chosen to make the question sharp: a ring of token cells resampled in place by the model's own windowed conditional p_r(x_i | x_{i+-r}). The substrate is Glauber dynamics on token sequences and is not new; what we change is the coupling. Advancing two rings that differ in one token under common random numbers makes undamaged copies diverge by exactly zero, so damage spreading becomes measurable where a maximal coupling gives mixing times instead. The answer is that it measures two different things at once, in readings that look alike. Some quantities are fixed by the construction: the damage light cone is kinematic, and the radius scaling of the token-space Lyapunov exponent lambda_ca(r) is model-invariant across 19 models and two scale ladders spanning 70x. Others genuinely track the model: lambda_ca crosses zero at a reproducible point in training, and the attractor share ranks models consistently however the lattice is built. Left undistinguished, the first kind is readily mistaken for the second -- we did so ourselves for four months, and report a phase transition we measured to three decimal places that belongs to the probe rather than to any language model. We give the test that separates them: hold the construction fixed and vary the model, or hold the model fixed and vary the construction, and see which readings move. We validate the instrument by reproduction first, recovering a Domany-Kinzel damage field bit-exactly against an independent prediction, and we report the estimator failures that this discipline caught -- four retracted verdicts, each on a quantity that looked like a measurement. The methodology ships as a package.

</details>


### [164] [ConRub-Med: Reinforcement Learning with Consensus Rubrics for Open-Ended Medical Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10996)
*Taojie Zhu,Yuan Xia,Tao Sun,Yizhi Wang,Yan Chen,Qunshan He,Tian Guan,Jian Wang,Jinjie Gu,Junwei Liu,Yonghong He*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为ConRub-Med的方法，通过模型生成可验证的评分细则（rubrics）并结合三态评分机制，在缺乏廉价结果验证的开放性医学问题中实现强化学习的有效奖励监督。该方法在多个医学基准测试中表现领先，并显著优于现有模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在数学和编程领域，强化学习可通过自动验证答案获得有效奖励，但在医学领域，由于缺乏低成本且可靠的结局验证手段，传统方法难以适用。医学回答常存在部分正确、不完整或临床错误等问题，依赖专家设计或审核评分标准成本高昂。因此，需要一种可扩展、具备临床合理性的自动化奖励构建方法。

Method: 提出ConRub-Med框架：首先由三个异构语言模型独立生成原子化评估标准；再由另一个模型整合，仅保留三者共同支持的标准；采用Three-State评分机制，分别评估正确内容、遗漏信息和错误陈述，其中错误给予负分；在策略优化阶段，使用Group Relative Policy Optimization（GRPO），当组内所有响应奖励相同时引入成对判断以提供排序优势，否则使用标准GRPO。

Result: 在盲法对照研究中，两名医学专家认为ConRub-Med生成的回答比单一生成器更具临床相关性；在九个基准任务中，ConRub-Med在六个上排名第一，医学和泛化性能平均得分最高；基于5,166个提示构成的评分数据集，其在HealthBench-Hard上得分为38.98±1.04，优于InfiMed-ORBIT在8,000和28,000样本下的表现（分别为33.60和37.30）。

Conclusion: ConRub-Med通过可验证、可扩展的模型生成评分细则，结合精细化的三态评分与改进的策略优化机制，能够在缺乏专家实时参与的情况下提升医学领域强化学习的效果，为开放性医学问题的自动评估与模型训练提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards has been especially effective in mathematics and coding, where answers can be checked automatically. Many open-ended medical questions lack comparably cheap outcome verifiers: responses may be partly correct, incomplete, or contain clinically consequential errors. Rubrics written or validated by physicians offer strong clinical grounding, but involving experts in every instance is costly. Model-generated rubrics make this supervision scalable. We introduce ConRub-Med to preserve useful distinctions as rubric feedback moves from construction to policy optimization. For each prompt, three heterogeneous language models propose atomic criteria independently; a separate model reviews them, retaining only criteria with semantic support from all three generators. Three-State scoring distinguishes correct coverage, missing information, and incorrect claims. Errors receive negative rather than zero credit. When every response in a complete Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) group receives the same final reward, a pairwise judge provides sequence advantages only if both candidate orders agree, without changing the scalar rewards. Groups without ties use vanilla GRPO. In a blinded study matched by question, two medical experts rate panels from the full pipeline as more clinically relevant than panels produced by one generator. Across the evaluated open models, ConRub-Med ranks first on six of nine benchmarks and achieves the highest medical and generalization averages. Using the resulting rubric dataset of 5,166 prompts, it scores $38.98 \pm 1.04$ (mean $\pm$ SD) on HealthBench-Hard, compared with InfiMed-ORBIT's 33.60 with 8,000 samples and 37.30 with 28,000.

</details>


### [165] [On the Limitations of Cross-Lingual Consistency in Multilingual Text-to-image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11002)
*Sicheng Zhang,Zhonghao Yan,Binzhu Xie,Shi Qiu,Muzammal Naseer,Naveed Akhtar,Mubarak Shah*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了LingT2I，一个涵盖10种语言、包含33K提示的文本到图像生成跨语言评测基准，揭示了模型在不同语言下的性能差异与语言依赖性问题，并探讨了语言因素及文化背景对生成结果的系统性影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本到图像生成研究主要集中于英文环境，缺乏对跨语言性能差距和语言特异性效应的充分探索。为此，作者希望构建一个全面的多语言基准以评估模型在非英语语境下的表现。

Method: 提出LingT2I基准，覆盖10种广泛使用的语言，包含33,000个提示，用于评估跨语言内容生成与文本渲染能力；在此基础上进行综合分析，量化语言间差异并识别生成模式。

Result: 发现显著的语言不平等现象和语言依赖的权衡效应（如内容保真度与文本可读性之间的冲突），并揭示了语言结构和文化背景对生成结果的系统性影响。

Conclusion: LingT2I为研究文本到图像模型的跨语言行为提供了基础，有助于推动更鲁棒、更具包容性的多语言生成模型的发展。

Abstract: Text-to-image (T2I) generation has achieved remarkable progress in recent years. However, existing research has largely focused on English-only settings, leaving cross-lingual performance gaps and language-specific effects insufficiently explored. To fill this gap, we introduce LingT2I, a benchmark covering 10 widely used languages with 33K prompts, designed to evaluate cross-lingual effects in both content generation and text rendering. Building on this benchmark, we conduct a comprehensive cross-lingual analysis, uncovering linguistic inequality and language-dependent trade-offs across evaluation dimensions. Beyond quantitative evaluation, we further reveal a range of language-dependent generation patterns, highlighting how linguistic factors and their corresponding cultural contexts systematically impact model outputs. Our benchmark and analysis provide a foundation for studying cross-lingual behavior in T2I generation and facilitate the development of more robust and inclusive models. Code and dataset are available at https://github.com/RISys-Lab/LingT2I.

</details>


### [166] [Templated or fully Synthetic? Prompt construction as a confound in measuring LLM political stance beyond writing assistance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11008)
*Ilias Chalkidis*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出使用完全合成的LLM生成提示来评估大语言模型的政治立场，相较于传统的模板化提示和真实提示，合成提示在现实性和意图传达上表现更优，并能减少评估偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有政治立场检测方法依赖于为人类设计的封闭式选择题或基于真实对话日志的模板化提示，缺乏真实交互中的细微差别，且易受刻意操纵影响，难以准确反映LLM在开放任务中的真实立场。

Method: 提出使用LLM生成的合成提示，以真实提示为种子，在详细指令下生成；通过小规模研究比较真实、模板化和LLM生成提示在3个政策议题和3个地缘冲突中的生态效度，由人类和LLM标注者进行现实性与意图清晰度评分，并开展案例研究分析不同提示对立场估计的影响。

Result: LLM生成的提示被评价为与真实提示同样真实、优于模板化提示，且更能清晰传达预期意图；LLM比人类更明显地区分模板化与其他两类提示；案例研究表明，模板化提示在中性语境下会高估模型的立场倾向。

Conclusion: LLM生成的合成提示在生态效度和立场检测准确性方面优于模板化提示，是更具潜力的政治立场评估工具，尤其适用于开放性任务场景。

Abstract: Political stance detection in LLMs has long been dominated by closed-ended, multiple-choice political survey questions---originally designed for humans, and thus lacks the realism and nuance of human-AI interactions in the wild, while also being susceptible to sandbagging. The recent IssueBench framework substantially mitigates these limitations with templated prompts anchored in real-world chat logs. Given the rise in non-work-related use of GenAI assistants, we extend IssueBench beyond writing assistance to include two additional tasks, information seeking and opinion sharing. We argue that templated prompts still lack the nuance of real ones, especially for open-ended tasks, and remain recognisable as evaluation artefacts. We propose the use of fully synthetic (LLM-generated) prompts, produced under detailed instructions with real prompts as seeds. We assess the ecological validity of real, templated, and LLM-generated prompts in a small-scale study covering 3 highly contested policy issues and 3 recent geopolitical conflicts. Human and LLM annotators rank LLM-generated prompts as no less realistic than real ones and clearly more realistic than templated ones, and find that they carry their intended intent and stance more clearly; the LLMs separate templated prompts from the other two far more sharply than the humans do. In a case study, templated and LLM-generated prompts yield systematically different stance estimates for the same model, most visibly under neutral framings, where templated prompts overstate the model's leaning in the direction encoded by the topic-and-stance text (filler) slotted into their templates.

</details>


### [167] [Data Attribution of Emergent Misalignment with Persona Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11025)
*Clemens Vetter,David Kaczér,Lucie Flek,Florian Mai*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了语言模型在微调过程中出现的新兴错位（EM）现象，探讨了其背后的机制，发现与预训练中获得的潜在特征（如越狱、欺骗等角色特征）有关，并指出仅语义相关的人类文本不足以引发EM，而合成的指令对则可以。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究语言模型在特定任务微调后为何会在无关领域表现出有害行为，特别是这些行为背后的机制是否源于预训练阶段形成的潜在特征。

Method: 使用基于稀疏自编码器（SAE）的模型差异分析方法，在四个开源权重模型上分析微调前后特征的变化，并通过引导单个特征来控制EM；同时将因果特征追溯到一百万个预训练网页文档，并测试人类撰写文本和合成指令对在微调中诱发EM的效果。

Result: 发现与越狱、讽刺、欺骗和操控相关的特征在错位微调中被放大，而安全性和助手身份特征被抑制；引导这些特征可使对齐模型的错位率高达62%（超过微调本身的35%），并能重新对齐错位模型；溯源发现相关特征与描述反派、支配和有害意图的文本相关，但仅用这些人类文本微调无法稳定引发EM，而由其生成的合成指令对可以且具跨模型泛化性。

Conclusion: 语义内容本身不足以引发新兴错位，响应结构或模型生成的语言风格在诱导EM中起关键作用，表明EM的产生依赖于形式与内容的结合。

Abstract: Emergent misalignment (EM) is the phenomenon where fine-tuning a language model on a narrow task leads to harmful behavior in unrelated domains. A leading mechanistic account attributes EM to persona features: latent directions acquired during pre-training that misaligned fine-tuning amplifies. We ask where these features come from: which pre-training documents activate them, and whether naturally occurring human-written text suffices to induce EM. Using Sparse Autoencoder (SAE) based model diffing across four open-weight models, we find that features related to jailbreak personas, sarcasm, deception, and manipulation are amplified by misalignment fine-tuning, while safety-relevant and assistant-identity features are suppressed. Steering individual features controls EM in both directions: it induces misalignment rates of up to 62% in aligned models -- exceeding the 35% reached by misalignment fine-tuning itself -- and re-aligns misaligned models to near-baseline misalignment rates. Attributing the causal features to a corpus of one million pre-training web documents retrieves semantically relevant narratives about villainous characters, domination, and harmful agency. However, fine-tuning on these human-written documents does not reliably induce EM, even after reformatting into assistant-style responses, whereas synthetic instruction-response pairs derived from the same content do -- and transfer across model families. Semantic relevance alone is therefore not sufficient: response structure or model-generated phrasing plays an important role in inducing EM.

</details>


### [168] [myMediWhisper: Construction of Burmese Medical Speech Corpus and Whisper Fine-Tuning for Clinical Dialogue ASR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11036)
*Ye Kyaw Thu,Ye Bhone Lin,Thura Aung,Htet Arkar,Myat Oo Swe,Thet Htet San,Min Thiha Tun,Thazin Myint Oo,Thepchai Supnithi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种基于高质量28小时缅甸语医疗语音语料库的语音识别框架，通过全量微调和LoRA参数高效微调优化Whisper模型，并评估了数据增强对噪声和混响环境下的鲁棒性影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管Whisper模型受益于大规模多语言预训练，但其在缅甸语医疗语音上的表现仍有限，因此需要针对该特定领域构建专用模型以提升性能。

Method: 构建了一个由母语者录制并验证的28小时高质量缅甸语医疗语音语料库，采用全量微调（FFT）和基于LoRA的参数高效微调（PEFT）方法优化Whisper模型，并在波形和频谱图层面应用数据增强技术以提高模型鲁棒性。

Result: 数据增强虽降低了干净语音的性能，但在噪声和混响环境中显著提升了模型鲁棒性；完全微调的myMediWhisper-Medium模型在无增强情况下取得了23.44%的词错误率（WER），达到当前最优水平，优于更大规模的通用领域微调模型。

Conclusion: 针对特定领域如缅甸语医疗语音，使用高质量专用语料库进行模型微调可显著提升语音识别性能，且在复杂声学环境下具备良好鲁棒性，展示了专业领域适配的重要性。

Abstract: Although Whisper models benefit from large-scale multilingual pre-training, their performance on Burmese medical speech remains limited. This work presents a Burmese medical speech recognition framework built on a high-quality 28-hour corpus recorded and validated by native speakers. We fine-tune Whisper models using full fine-tuning (FFT) and parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) with LoRA. To evaluate robustness, we apply waveform- and spectrogram-level data augmentation under controlled noise and simulated room acoustics. While augmentation reduces performance on clean speech, it significantly improves robustness in noisy and reverberant environments across FFT and PEFT settings. Our best-performing system, fully fine-tuned myMediWhisper-Medium without augmentation, achieves a state-of-the-art Word Error Rate (WER) of 23.44%, outperforming much larger general-domain fine-tuned models. Dataset and other resources can be found at the Huggingface repository: https://huggingface.co/datasets/LULab/mediTalk-mm-rdy.

</details>


### [169] [TEAMMix: Taxonomy Enrichment Augmentation and Minority-augmented Mixing Strategy for LLM-enhanced Weak-Supervised Hierarchical Text Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11044)
*Jian Zhang,Zhuohao Yang,Songlin Lei,Bangli Liu,Ziwei Wang,Xufeng Weng,Gehan Amaratunga,Yu Lin,Hongwei Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于大语言模型（LLM）数据增强的弱监督分层文本分类框架，通过语义扩展标签层次结构和生成伪样本缓解长尾问题，显著提升了细粒度和不平衡数据集上的分类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于大语言模型的方法在分层文本分类中面临提示过长、标签结构信息丢失以及类别不平衡等问题，难以有效应用。

Method: 首先通过关键词生成和语料库挖掘丰富标签层次的语义信息；然后利用LLM生成伪样本以缓解长尾问题，并采用高斯混合模型进行基于置信度的重采样以优化生成数据质量。

Result: 实验结果表明，该方法有效提高了LLM生成伪标签的可靠性，在多个细粒度和不平衡数据集上显著提升了分类性能。

Conclusion: 所提出的弱监督框架结合LLM数据增强和标签结构优化，能够有效应对分层文本分类中的复杂标签结构和类别不平衡挑战。

Abstract: Hierarchical Text Classification (HTC), as a critical text mining task, faces challenges such as complex label hierarchies and class imbalance. Existing methods based on large language models (LLMs) struggle to be efficiently applied to this task due to issues like lengthy prompts and loss of label structural information. To address these limitations, this paper proposes a weakly supervised HTC framework enhanced by LLM-based data augmentation. The framework first enriches the label hierarchy semantically through keyword generation and corpus mining, thereby enhancing the model's understanding of labels. Subsequently, it guides the LLM to generate pseudo-samples to mitigate the long-tail problem, and employs a Gaussian mixture model for confidence-based resampling to optimize the quality of generated data. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed method effectively improves the reliability of LLM-generated pseudo-labels and significantly enhances classification performance on fine-grained and imbalanced datasets.

</details>


### [170] [Multiclass Sentiment Analysis for Identifying Political Viewpoints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11049)
*Girma Yohannis Bade,Olga Kolesnikova,Jose Luis Oropeza,Grigori Sidorov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了社交媒体上政治观点的多类别情感分析，提出了基于XGBoost和BERT的两种机器学习方法，并在标注数据集上进行了评估，实验结果显示两类模型的F1分数均较低，表明复杂政治语境下情感分类的挑战性，为未来多类别政治情感分析研究提供了基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于社交媒体中政治话语快速增长，蕴含大量公众意见，通过情感分析技术自动识别不同政治立场和情绪变得尤为重要。现有方法在处理复杂、语境化强的政治文本时表现有限，因此需要探索更有效的多类别情感分类方法。

Method: 设计并比较了两种机器学习方法：一种是基于特征工程的XGBoost模型，另一种是基于预训练语言模型BERT的深度学习方法；使用标注的政治社交媒体帖子数据集进行训练与评估，采用标准分类指标（如F1分数）衡量性能。

Result: XGBoost模型在测试集上达到0.2835的F1分数，BERT模型达到0.2806的F1分数，两者表现相近但整体较低，反映出政治情感分类任务的高难度。

Conclusion: 当前模型在处理语境复杂的政治情感文本时仍面临重大挑战，现有的结果可作为未来多类别政治情感分析研究的基准。

Abstract: The rapid growth of social media has created vast amounts of political discourse, which provides valuable opportunities to analyze public opinions and identify different political perspectives. Sentiment Analysis (SA) is a core task in Natural Language Processing (NLP) that allows the computational study of attitudes and opinions in textual data, and has become increasingly important for understanding political discourse. In this work, we investigate multiclass sentiment analysis of political view- points on social media, that is to automatically discriminate multiple sentiment classes over political issues and figures. To solve this task we design and evaluate two machine-learning approaches based on XGBoost and BERT. We train and evaluate the models on a labeled dataset of political social media posts using standard classification metrics. The experimental results show that the XGBoost model reaches an F1-score of 0.2835 and the BERT- based model reaches an F1-score of 0.2806 on the test set. These results demonstrate the challenge of classifying complex and contextualized political discourse sentiment and provide a baseline for future research in multiclass political sentiment analysis.

</details>


### [171] [Actions Speak Louder than Words: Measuring Cross-Lingual Policy Retention in Tool-Using Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11110)
*Sourabrata Mukherjee,Kalika Bali,Sunayana Sitaram*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了多语言环境下工具使用代理的行为一致性问题，发现不同语言下的行动策略存在显著差异，并揭示了现有评估方法的缺陷。经过修正后，大型模型在跨语言任务中表现出较高的行为一致性，而小模型则受随机因素影响较大。此外，代理倾向于通过英语作为中介语言处理非英语任务，且某些评估失败实际上源于提取轨迹的正则表达式设计问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究多语言场景下工具使用代理在执行相同任务时是否采取相同步骤，关注其行动策略的一致性，而非仅比较最终答案。强调行动本身的重要性，因其直接影响系统成本、延迟、失败模式和可审计性。

Method: 对8个模型、6个并行基准和41种语言（共238万条轨迹）进行大规模评估，识别并消除五种干扰因素：短轨迹偏见、空轨迹完美得分、偶然一致性、模型自身可重复性限制以及同一语言下重复提问的回答不一致。采用归一化方法控制模型自身可重复性，并分析不同解码策略（如贪心解码和温度变化）下的表现。

Result: 纠正所有干扰因素后，发现跨语言行动策略的差异是结构性的而非采样噪声所致；四个前沿大模型在贪心解码下保持71-73%的跨语言行为一致性，模型身份仅解释5.7%的方差；小于10B参数的模型表现下降，排序主要由随机基线决定；代理普遍将非英语任务通过英语中转，且该行为具有因果重要性；一个简单的轨迹提取正则表达式错误导致观测到的多语言失败，实际模型输出准确性并未显著变化。

Conclusion: 跨语言任务中的行动策略差异是系统性的，大型模型展现出较强的跨语言行为稳定性，而小模型易受随机因素干扰；当前多语言评估存在方法论缺陷，需更严谨地归一化和控制变量；代理依赖英语作为枢纽语言的现象普遍存在且难以纠正；部分报告的失败可能源于评估工具而非模型本身。

Abstract: When a tool-using agent is given the same task in a different language, does it still take the same steps? Multilingual evaluation rarely asks: it compares final answers and discards the actions. Yet those actions are the product: they fix cost and latency, decide how the system fails, and are the only auditable part of its behaviour. We make the action policy the measured object across 8 models, 6 parallel benchmarks and 41 languages (2.38M rollouts). The naive measurement fails: five confounds sit between raw trace similarity and any defensible claim, each able to flip a conclusion. Short traces score higher, empty traces score perfectly, unrelated traces agree by chance over half the time, the gap is capped by each model's reproducibility, and a model asked the same question twice in one language answers differently, leaving no baseline. We remove all five, and every correction makes the effect larger. Divergence proves structural, not sampling noise: it survives greedy decoding in every cell and stays flat as temperature rises, even as models grow less self-consistent. Normalised by their own reproducibility, four very different frontier models converge under greedy decoding, each keeping 71-73% of its action policy across languages, with model identity explaining only 5.7% of the variance. Below roughly 10B parameters it breaks down, and the ordering among smaller models is largely an artifact of a chance floor we measure by permutation rather than assume. Agents route non-English tasks through English; this pivot is causally load-bearing, confirmed by a pre-registered prediction across four models, and models will not abandon it when told to. Finally, a single trace-extraction regex, not the model, manufactured a multilingual failure: two worked examples raise one model's measured accuracy twenty-sixfold while its accuracy on readable outputs barely moves.

</details>


### [172] [Attention-Path Fragility as an Uncertainty Signal in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11138)
*Minsoo Kim,Sungyoung Ji,Kisung Moon,Ilyong Yoon*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出ASMI（Attention-Subnetwork Mutual Information）作为无需训练的不确定性估计方法，通过扰动注意力头并测量子网络间的互信息来识别模型预测的脆弱性，尤其在基于上下文的问答任务中优于现有方法，且能自动判断适用范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统置信度度量难以准确反映模型在特定输入下的不确定性，尤其是当高置信预测仍可能出错时；因此需要一种新的训练-free方法来更好捕捉模型预测的脆弱性。

Method: 提出ASMI方法，通过遮蔽不同注意力头生成多个子网络，计算这些子网络输出之间的BALD互信息，并引入语义一致性核函数以忽略表面形式差异；进一步设计Sem-ASMI实现单次生成即可评估。

Result: ASMI在12个基于上下文的问答基准-主干组合中，有10个优于或持平语义熵；自适应变体在8个设置中达到最佳表现，其中3个显著胜出；在参数化问答中性能退化至MSP基线水平，符合理论预期；结果具有高度可重复性。

Conclusion: ASMI是一种有效的训练-free不确定性估计方法，能够识别‘高置信但脆弱’的错误预测，在依赖上下文推理的任务中表现优异，并能自我界定适用边界，为可信AI提供实用工具。

Abstract: We propose that a model's uncertainty about a token is reflected not only in the breadth of its output distribution but also in whether a confident prediction is \emph{fragile} under perturbation of its attention pathways. We instantiate this as ASMI (Attention-Subnetwork Mutual Information), a training-free estimator that masks attention heads and measures the BALD mutual information among the resulting subnetworks, with a semantic-agreement kernel to discount surface-form disagreement. The signal is not a restatement of output confidence: on grounded QA an out-of-fold test shows it adds error-predictive information beyond single-pass confidence and entropy, concentrated in \emph{confident-but-fragile} predictions, where acting on it roughly halves the retained error of a confidence filter. The distinctness is regime-graded, so ASMI predicts its own domain of applicability, strong where answers are routed through provided context and bounded by design where they are recalled from parametric knowledge. Sem-ASMI reads the signal from a single greedy response, without the stochastic generations the strongest baselines require, and ties or beats Semantic Entropy on ten of the twelve grounded benchmark-backbone settings. Across the same twelve settings, the best ASMI variant, typically the adaptive one reusing the ten samples already drawn for the baselines, ties or leads the strongest baseline in eight, significantly in three under a paired test. On parametric QA all variants revert to or below the zero-cost MSP baseline, exactly as predicted, and the estimates are near-deterministic across reruns. A head-level analysis shows that what tracks this boundary is not the presence of head-level fragility but whether that fragility couples to errors.

</details>


### [173] [The Illusion of Cross-Lingual Safety in Low-Resource Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11146)
*Abigail Oppong,P Sam Sahil,Tadesse Destaw Belay,Maryam Ibrahim Mukhtar,Esmael Ahmed Abdu,Tassallah Abdullahi,Jessica Oparebea,Saminu Mohammad Aliyu,Idris Abdulmumin,Abubakar Juma Chilala,Nicholaus Dismas Ladislaus,Alfred Malengo Kondoro,Lemofouet Valdini Douglace,Shamsuddeen Hassan Muhammad,Seid Muhie Yimam*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了大语言模型在低资源非洲语言中的跨语言安全对齐问题，发现当前的安全机制在这些语言中表现严重不足，表明多语言安全对齐具有表面性，无法实现通用的语言无关伤害检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于当前大语言模型的安全对齐主要基于英语开发，假设其可泛化到多语言环境，但这一假设在低资源语言中尚未充分验证，存在安全隐患。

Method: 研究使用新构建的LoDNA数据集（包含四种非洲语言的直译与文化本地化提示），并提出一种基于隐藏状态拒绝表征的潜在几何分析框架，以超越传统的生成式评估方法。

Result: 实验显示跨语言安全传递效果极差，有害提示在大多数语言-模型组合中仅保留不到10%的英语拒绝信号；直译与本地化提示语义高度相似（余弦相似度0.95–0.996），但在模型深层中出现表征漂移。

Conclusion: 当前的多语言安全对齐机制是表面的，缺乏真正的语言无关伤害识别能力，尤其在所研究的低资源非洲语言中，不能假设存在通用的伤害流形。

Abstract: Safety alignment in large language models (LLMs) is largely developed in English, assuming these safeguards generalize across multilingual settings. However, this assumption remains underexplored and exposes a vulnerability in low-resource languages. We investigate cross-lingual safety transfer in four African languages, Twi, Hausa, Amharic, and Swahili, using LoDNA, a new safety dataset that pairs literal translations with culturally localized prompts. To move beyond generation-based evaluation, we propose a latent geometric framework that probes hidden-state refusal representations in LLMs. Our experimental results show that cross-lingual safety transfer is severely limited; harmful prompts retain less than 10% of the English refusal signal across most language-model pairs. Literal and localized prompts are semantically aligned (cosine 0.95-0.996) but drift across layers, suggesting models encode the concepts without routing them to safety mechanisms. These findings demonstrate that current multilingual safety alignment is superficial, providing strong evidence against the assumption of a universal, language-agnostic harm manifold within the specific low-resource languages studied. Warning: This paper contains example data that may be offensive or harmful.

</details>


### [174] [From Interpretability to Control: Insights from Six Years of the TrustNLP Workshop](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11171)
*Rahul Gupta,Abhinav Mohanty,Anaelia Ovalle,Anil Ramakrishna,Anubrata Das,Apurv Verma,Jwala Dhamala,Ninareh Mehrabi,Tharindu Kumarage,Yada Pruksachatkun,Yang Trista Cao,Kai-Wei Chang,Aram Galstyan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: TrustNLP研讨会自2021年起记录了自然语言处理领域向生成系统可信赖性的转变，通过对144篇论文的分类分析，揭示了真实性、公平性和可解释性等六个信任维度的发展趋势，并指出未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着生成模型的发展，确保其可信性成为关键问题，需要系统性地理解各信任维度的演变和相互关系。

Method: 基于TrustLLM和DecodingTrust框架，对六届TrustNLP共144篇论文进行分类，分析六大信任维度的演变趋势，并与ACL等主要会议进行跨会议比较。

Result: 发现真实性是增长最快的信任维度（从无到占37%），公平性最稳定，可解释性呈U型变化；首个高影响力聊天模型的发布同时激活所有信任维度，后续模型更关注真实性和安全性；TrustNLP主题分布与主流会议基本一致。

Conclusion: 提出了四个结构性洞察，为可信NLP研究社区提供了可操作的未来方向，强调需加强机制性理解和主动控制。

Abstract: The Workshop on Trustworthy Natural Language Processing (TrustNLP), co-located with major ACL conferences since 2021, has grown from 8 proceedings papers to 41 over six editions, documenting a field-wide transition from post-hoc interpretability of static models to mechanistic understanding and proactive control of generative systems. We synthesize insights from all 144 proceedings papers, classifying them along six trust dimensions grounded in established frameworks (TrustLLM, DecodingTrust). We observe co-occurrences with capability emergence. The release of the first high-impact chat models activated all trust dimensions simultaneously, while subsequent model generations shifted focus toward truthfulness and safety alignment. Analysis from the classification study reveals that truthfulness is the fastest-growing dimension (absent in 2021-2022, comprising 37% of papers by 2025-2026), fairness remains the most consistent theme, and explainability exhibits a U-shaped trajectory; declining as post-hoc methods lost relevance but resurging in 2026 through mechanistic interpretability. A cross-venue comparison with ACL, NAACL, EACL, and EMNLP (~2K papers) in the same period shows that TrustNLP's topical distribution closely follows the field average. We identify four structural insights and conclude with actionable directions for the research community.

</details>


### [175] [ConVAWG: A Retrieval-Grounded Framework for Controlled Synthetic Dialogue Generation in Violence Against Women and Girls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11200)
*Chen Lyu,Xingwei Tan,Simon Cullen,Shelley Wilson,Lois Arthurs,Arshad Jhumka,Gabriele Pergola*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于生成与犯罪模式相符的合成家庭暴力对话数据集ConVAWG的框架，旨在克服真实对话数据在隐私和法律上的获取难题，并支持对针对妇女和女孩暴力行为的动态建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于隐私和法律限制，真实的大规模敏感对话数据难以获取和发布，现有研究多集中于句子级别的毒性检测，缺乏对虐待行为作为关系性和时间性现象的建模。因此，需要一种能够模拟暴力行为动态演变过程的方法。

Method: 提出ConVAWG框架，结合人物设定种子、英国国家统计局的人口统计模式、官方犯罪定义以及家庭杀人审查案例，构建多层次事件时间线，生成多场景角色扮演对话，并通过定向激活引导的毒性控制机制调控对话中的有害内容。

Result: 发布了包含200个情境、超过6000个多轮对话事件的数据集，配备丰富的场景、事件和语句层级元数据；通过人工评估、LLM-as-Judge评测、消融实验和下游任务验证了生成对话的高质量和领域保真度。

Conclusion: ConVAWG能够有效生成符合现实犯罪模式的合成VAWG对话数据，为研究敏感领域中的对话动态提供了可扩展且合规的新途径。

Abstract: Synthetic dialogue generation offers a way to study conversational dynamics in sensitive domains where real data are difficult to access, release, or annotate. The underlying abuse may occur online or offline: threats and coercion can appear directly in messages, while behaviours such as surveillance, isolation, stalking, and physical violence may be planned, disclosed, or referred to conversationally. Privacy and legal constraints make it difficult the release of large-scale real conversation datasets; existing work has mostly focused on sentence-level toxicity of online abuses, leaving a gap in modelling abuse as a relational and temporally unfolding phenomenon. In this work, we focus on modelling Violence Against Women and Girls (VAWG) scenarios as multi-turn dialogues. We introduce ConVAWG, a retrieval-grounded framework for generating CPS-aligned synthetic VAWG chat dialogues. ConVAWG builds scenarios from persona seeds, demographic patterns reported by the UK Office for National Statistics, official crime definitions, and retrieved Domestic Homicide Review cases; converts them into hierarchical event timelines; generates multi-scene role-play dialogues; and applies targeted activation-steered toxicity control to appropriate utterances. We release over 6,000 multi-turn dialogue events across 200 scenarios with rich scenario-, event-, and turn-level metadata. Extensive human evaluation, LLM-as-Judge assessment, ablations, and downstream tasks show strong dialogue quality and domain fidelity.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [176] [Protection Levels for Vision-Based Pose Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10023)
*Olivia Beyer Bruvik,Romeo Valentin,Marc R. Schlichting,Don Walker,Mykel J. Kochenderfer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于航空环境下非线性Perspective-n-Point问题的保护水平计算算法，直接覆盖飞机姿态的全部六个自由度，并分析了测量冗余、像素级预测不确定性和跑道距离对保护水平的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了在基于视觉导航中提供类似接收机自主完好性监测的完整性保证，特别是在存在未检测到故障的情况下仍能确保姿态估计的可靠性。

Method: 扩展了先前的概率性计算机视觉框架，推导出在未检测到故障下仍有效的姿态误差概率边界（即保护水平），并针对航空环境中的Perspective-n-Point问题设计了相应的计算算法。

Result: 提出了可计算六自由度姿态保护水平的算法，分析了测量冗余、预测不确定性和跑道距离对保护水平的影响，并通过示例展示了保护水平之间的权衡关系。

Conclusion: 该方法为基于视觉的航空导航提供了具有完整性保障的姿态估计方案，能够在存在潜在故障时仍提供可信的姿态误差界限。

Abstract: Vision-based navigation complements Global Navigation Satellite Systems, but certification demands integrity guarantees that account for faulty measurements. Previous work presented a probabilistic computer vision pipeline for runway-based pose estimation with fault detection inspired by Receiver Autonomous Integrity Monitoring. This work extends that framework by deriving protection levels, which provide probabilistic bounds on pose error that remain valid under undetected faults. We present an algorithm for computing protection levels for the nonlinear Perspective-$n$-Point problem applied to an aviation setting. The algorithm covers all six degrees of freedom of the aircraft pose (position and orientation) directly. We analyze the effect of measurement redundancy, pixel-level prediction uncertainty, and runway distance on the resulting protection levels. To make the results tangible, we demonstrate tradeoffs in the protection levels on an illustrative runway example.

</details>


### [177] [The Impact of Operational-Data Fidelity when Assessing Safety-Critical Autonomous-Vehicle Software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10025)
*Kizito Salako,Rabiu Tsoho Muhammad*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文扩展了保守贝叶斯推断技术，提出一种用于评估基于不充分详细操作数据所得软件可靠性结论的稳健性的统计方法，并揭示了在自动驾驶汽车安全评估中，数据粒度不足可能导致过于乐观的可靠性判断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于安全关键软件的操作历史数据可能缺乏对故障事件的足够细节描述，导致可靠性评估无法充分考虑重要故障特征，因此需要一种能够评估此类评估结果稳健性的方法。

Method: 通过扩展保守贝叶斯推断（CBI）技术，构建了一种原则性的统计方法，用以分析操作数据细粒度不足对可靠性声称的影响，并给出数据保真度影响的保守估计。

Result: 研究表明，在自动驾驶车辆安全评估场景中，不够精细的操作数据会严重削弱可靠性声称的有效性，即使评估者试图保守使用这些数据，仍可能导致危险的乐观结论。

Conclusion: 低保真度数据的保守使用可能是天真的，必须重视数据细粒度对软件可靠性评估的影响，本文提供了首个关于数据保真度影响的保守估计方法。

Abstract: For safety-critical software, data from the software's operational past (e.g. a sequence of success and failure events experienced by the software) can provide strong statistical support for reliability claims about the software. However, such data might not describe past software failure events in sufficient detail, and this might leave a reliability assessment (based on this data) unable to account for important features of past software failures. In this paper, by extending conservative Bayesian inference (CBI) techniques used in reliability assessment, we illustrate a principled statistical approach for checking the robustness of reliability claims derived from insufficiently detailed operational data. We demonstrate the extent to which insufficient detail in operational data can undermine software reliability claims in autonomous vehicle (AV) safety assessment scenarios. Reliability claims derived from insufficiently fine-grained data might be dangerously optimistic, despite a concerted effort by an assessor to use such data conservatively during the assessment. While these findings are consistent with previous work on the impact of statistical model fidelity in Bayesian software reliability assessments, our work clarifies why attempts to use low-fidelity data conservatively can be naive, and we give the first conservative estimates of the impact of data fidelity on assessments.

</details>


### [178] [Navigating the Proximity-Safety Balance: Constraint Decomposition for Human Following in Pedestrian Crowds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10056)
*Shiting Gong,Jianpeng Yao,Jinfeng Wang,Marco Pavone,Jiachen Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多约束强化学习方法，用于在拥挤环境中实现机器人跟随目标人的任务，通过分解任务奖励和成本约束，显式控制接近性与安全性的权衡，并结合人类运动预测的不确定性估计提升鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在密集人群中跟随目标人时，保持接近与确保安全之间存在固有冲突，传统强化学习方法难以灵活调节这一权衡。

Method: 将跟随任务分解为稀疏任务奖励和多个独立的成本约束，采用多约束强化学习框架，并引入基于预测不确定性的成本调整机制。

Result: 实验表明，该方法在分布内和分布外场景下均能有效平衡接近性与安全性，优于基线方法，并在真实机器人部署中验证了可行性。

Conclusion: 所提方法通过显式的成本约束和不确定性感知机制，实现了可调节、安全且鲁棒的人类跟随策略。

Abstract: Following a target human in crowded environments involves an inherent conflict between staying close to the target and navigating safely among surrounding pedestrians and obstacles. This conflict becomes more severe in dense scenarios, where aggressive following risks collisions and conservative margins lead to target loss, especially when pedestrian behaviors are unfamiliar or unpredictable. Existing reinforcement learning (RL) methods typically encode these competing objectives into a single dense reward, but the resulting proximity-safety balance is implicit and difficult to adjust across conditions. To address this, we decompose the human-following task into a sparse task reward and independent cost constraints within a multi-constraint RL formulation, where each constraint is managed through cost thresholds with direct behavioral meaning rather than implicit reward weight ratios, allowing explicit and tunable control over the trade-off. We further quantify the prediction uncertainty of human motions and integrate these estimates into the RL costs to enhance safety under unpredictable conditions. Extensive experiments across both in-distribution and out-of-distribution settings demonstrate that our method achieves an effective proximity-safety balance compared to baselines. Real-robot deployment further validates the feasibility of our method in real-world scenarios. More details are available on our project page: https://nav-ps-balance.github.io/.

</details>


### [179] [Whole-Body Planning for Humanoids Navigating Confined Spaces via Self-Collision Avoidance References](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10220)
*Carlos Gonzalez,Luis Sentis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种三阶段全身规划框架，通过在可运动刚体体积上直接进行运动学路径规划，并结合可微分碰撞规避，生成高质量的长期轨迹，用于训练强化学习策略以实现人形机器人在高度受限环境中的稳定行走。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统轨迹优化器在高度受限环境中难以有效处理复杂的碰撞空间和自碰撞约束，容易陷入局部极小，导致规划失败。

Method: 提出三阶段框架：1）基于可运动刚体体积的运动学路径规划；2）引入可微分碰撞规避的可达性约束优化，生成体积感知的引导轨迹；3）使用优化轨迹作为参考，训练残差强化学习策略以实现鲁棒在线执行。

Result: 在Unitree G1人形机器人上验证，跨越三个符合NIST应急响应标准的测试场景（限制比Cr < 1.5），成功生成12至18秒含复杂手脚接触的可行轨迹，标准基线方法失败；在物理仿真中经大量域随机化后，学习策略能成功跟踪轨迹。

Conclusion: 该框架显著提升了人形机器人在极端狭窄环境下的运动规划能力，结合优化与学习方法实现了动态可行、抗干扰的在线运动控制。

Abstract: Humanoid locomotion in highly confined environments requires navigating dense environmental obstacles and complex self-collision bounds while maintaining multi-contact dynamic feasibility. Traditional trajectory optimizers frequently struggle in these restricted spaces, as navigating the large collision space with splines on particle abstractions is insufficient and leads to poor local minima. To address this, we propose a three-stage whole-body planning framework that formulates kinematic path planning directly over kinematically reachable rigid-body volumes. By integrating differentiable collision avoidance into a reachability-constrained formulation, our framework synthesizes volume-informed guides that reliably guide a full-order trajectory optimizer over long horizons. We show that these optimized plans serve as high-quality references to train a residual reinforcement learning policy for robust online execution. We validate our approach on the Unitree G1 humanoid across three benchmark testbeds exceeding NIST emergency response standards, achieving restricted confinement ratios ($C_r < 1.5$). Our framework generates feasible trajectories across 12-to-18-second tasks with complex foot and hand contacts where standard baselines fail, while the learned policy successfully tracks these plans under extensive domain randomization in physics simulation.

</details>


### [180] [FACT: Failure-Aware Causal Training for World-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10232)
*Quanquan Peng,Yutong Liang,Rui Yan,Nicklas Hansen,Xiaolong Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种因果世界-动作模型FACT，通过引入失败动作的回放来监督动作结果，提升了对未来预测的鲁棒性，并在模拟和真实双臂操作任务中表现出优于现有基线的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的世界-动作模型主要基于成功演示进行训练，难以预测不良动作的后果，导致未来预测存在成功偏差。本文旨在通过引入失败动作数据，增强模型对动作结果的理解能力。

Method: 提出FACT模型，采用动作条件接口，利用失败回放作为未来预测的监督信号，同时预测未来视频和任务进展；通过逆动力学模型或目标条件生成动作，并在推理时可选地对采样动作进行评分。

Result: 在模拟和真实双臂操作任务上，FACT优于多个基线模型，随着失败数据的加入性能持续提升，并减少了在不良动作下的成功偏差幻觉。

Conclusion: FACT通过整合失败动作数据实现了更全面的动作后果预测，在复杂操作任务中展现了更强的泛化能力和实用性。

Abstract: Recent world-action models (WAMs) show that co-training policies with future prediction can provide physical priors for action generation. Building on the future-prediction ability of video models, many WAMs generate future videos and recover actions with inverse-dynamics models, or use these predicted videos as goal conditions for action generation. In both cases, the world model is trained mostly on successful demonstrations and has little reason to predict the consequences of bad actions. We introduce FACT, a causal World-Action Model that predicts future video and task progress conditioned on the executed action. This action-conditioned interface allows failure rollouts to supervise action consequences, turning bad actions into valid future targets rather than being discarded. Failure-aware training makes the progress predictor aware of both successful and failed action outcomes, which can optionally be used to score sampled action candidates at inference. Extensive experiments on simulation and real-world bimanual manipulation tasks show that FACT outperforms many existing baselines, improves as failure data are incorporated into training, and reduces success-biased future hallucination under bad actions. See more details at https://fact-wam.github.io/

</details>


### [181] [A Neural Network Based Teleoperation for Remote Controlled Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10367)
*Ning Ding,Azim Eskandarian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于波变量和自适应RBF网络的单边遥操作框架，用于解决通信延迟和环境干扰下的车辆遥操作问题，具有低计算开销、高鲁棒性，并通过仿真与硬件实验验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接遥操作车辆面临通信延迟和操作者无法感知未建模环境扰动（如空气阻力、路面倾斜）的问题，同时轮胎-路面非线性动力学加剧控制难度，现有方法在稳定性、适应性和计算效率之间难以平衡。

Method: 采用波变量（WV）方法保证在随机时延下的无源稳定性，并结合自适应径向基函数网络（RBFN）补偿车辆特有的不确定性；设计了解耦的自适应律以分别处理纵向和横向动力学，且采用模型无关的RBFN实现快速在线适应，降低计算负担。

Result: 仿真对比PID、LQR、MPC和NMPC表明，该方法在抗未建模干扰方面表现更优，且执行时间远低于MPC和NMPC；硬件在环实验基于1/10比例车辆和4G网络，验证了系统在真实道路不确定性下的可行性、安全性和轨迹跟踪鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架在保证稳定性和安全性的同时，实现了高性能、低延迟和强适应性的车辆遥操作，适用于资源受限的边缘计算场景，为实际部署提供了可行解决方案。

Abstract: Direct teleoperation of vehicles faces critical technical bottlenecks: communication latency and the operator's inability to physically perceive unmodeled environmental disturbances (e.g., aerodynamic drag, bank angles) coupled with highly nonlinear tire-road dynamics. To address these challenges, we propose a tailored unilateral teleoperation framework. The system integrates the Wave Variable (WV) approach to passively guarantee stability under stochastic delays, and an adaptive Radial Basis Function Network (RBFN) to actively compensate for vehicle-specific uncertainties. Unlike existing WV-neural network architectures designed for bilateral robotic arms, our framework features decoupled adaptive laws specifically designed for vehicle longitudinal and lateral dynamics. Furthermore, compared to model-heavy predictive controllers, the model-free RBFN offers rapid online adaptation without heavy computational overhead. Building upon our preliminary theoretical formulation, this brief paper presents comprehensive comparative analyses and real-world hardware validations. Simulation benchmarks against PID, LQR, MPC, and NMPC demonstrate that the RBFN achieves superior robustness against unmodeled disturbances while requiring orders of magnitude less execution time than MPC and NMPC, making it ideal for resource-constrained vehicle edge computing. Finally, hardware-in-the-loop experiments using a 1/10th scale vehicle over a 4G network validate the system's practical feasibility, safety, and robust trajectory tracking under physical road uncertainties.

</details>


### [182] [Real-World Cooperative Bimanual Dexterous Grasp of Large Objects from Single-View Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10383)
*Ziming Li,Mingxuan Wu,Jiaqi Zhang,Hongfei Li,Yan Gan,Deqiang Ouyang,Ning Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种真实世界双臂抓取框架，通过多模态数据和去噪扩散模型从单视角点云生成可执行的双臂抓取配置，实现了对未知物体的高成功率抓取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多集中于顺序操作而非协同抓取，且缺乏完整的3D物体模型和物理可行的抓取动作生成方法，限制了双臂灵巧抓取在真实场景中的应用。

Method: 构建包含关节角度、视觉观测和力信号的多模态数据集；采用基于DDPM的模块从分割点云生成关节级抓取配置；结合运动规划与在线抓取优化策略以确保物理稳定性和可行性。

Result: 在双臂机器人上的实验显示对不同几何形状和位姿的未见物体具有高抓取成功率，消融实验证明了系统各关键组件的有效性。

Conclusion: 该方法降低了对完整3D模型的依赖，实现了从单视角输入合成可在现实世界稳定执行的双臂抓取动作，推动了双臂协同抓取在实际场景中的应用。

Abstract: Bimanual dexterous grasping of large objects is a critical challenge in robotic manipulation. However, most existing studies focus on sequential manipulation rather than cooperative grasping, and methods addressing such bimanual tasks have largely been limited to simulation. These limitations stem from the difficulty of acquiring full 3D object models and generating physically plausible grasping actions. To fill this gap, we propose a real-world bimanual grasping framework that includes: a multimodal dataset capturing joint angles, visual observations and force signals; a Denoising Diffusion Probabilistic Model (DDPM)-based module that generates joint-level grasp configurations from segmented point clouds; and an execution strategy that integrates motion planning with online grasp refinement to ensure physical stability and feasibility. Our approach enables the synthesis of executable bimanual grasps from single-view inputs, reducing dependence on complete 3D object models and ensuring stable real-world performance. Experiments on a dual-arm robot demonstrate high success rates across unseen objects with varying geometries and poses, and ablation studies confirm the contributions of key components of our system.

</details>


### [183] [Hip Energized Monopedal Hopping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10387)
*Shane Rozen-Levy,Griffon McMahon,Daniel Koditschek*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种新的步态控制策略，利用俯仰稳定产生的反作用力矩来补偿能量损耗，并通过调整质心位置和能量分配实现稳定的平面单足跳跃运动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的PD+前馈控制器在稳定俯仰时会产生反应扭矩，这些扭矩通常被视为干扰；本文希望利用这些扭矩来补偿阻尼引起的能量损失，从而提升运动效率和稳定性。

Method: 通过移动质心位置增加俯仰稳定扭矩以向系统输入能量，并设计新的落脚点策略调节径向与角向自由度间的能量分布，结合混合平均法分析获得步态的固定点和特征值解析表达式。

Result: 在五连杆双足模型和Penn Jerboa机器人上的仿真显示分析预测与实际表现一致；物理实验中，Jerboa实现了1.02–1.77 m/s（5.10–8.85倍腿长/秒）的稳定奔跑。

Conclusion: 该方法有效利用原本被浪费的反应扭矩来维持步态能量，结合理论分析与实验验证，展示了在无俯仰锁定的平面单足机器人上实现高效稳定运动的可行性。

Abstract: We present a novel stepping strategy for pitch unlocked planar monopeds where the reaction torques from stabilizing pitch with a conventional PD + feedfoward controller are recruited to counteract energetic losses from damping. By moving the location of the mass center, our controller increases the pitch stabilization torque, thereby adding energy to the gait. A new stepping policy adjusts the distribution of energy between the radial and angular degrees of freedom to counteract dissipative losses and achieve a user specified balance between steady state fore-aft speed and apex height. Hybrid averaging analysis yields closed form expressions for the fixed points and eigenvalues of the resulting gait, lending insight into the interplay between the physical and control parameters' influence on performance. Simulation studies on a generic 5 link biped and a careful model of the Penn Jerboa reveal a useful correspondence to these analytical predictions. Physical experiments on the Penn Jerboa exhibit stable locomotion with speeds ranging from 1.02 m/s to 1.77 m/s (5.10 leg lengths/s to 8.85 leg lengths/s) in a manner effectively approximated by the mathematical analysis.

</details>


### [184] [PBD-AG: Persistent Baseline-Delta Active Graphs with Uncertainty-Aware Inspection for Long-Horizon Service Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10449)
*Shuo Bao,Wei Dong,Shuyue Zhang,Ming Shang,Yuchen Huang,Han Yu,Chengjie Xu,Yiheng Bi,Kai Sun,Fuchun Sun,Xinzhou Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PBD-AG是一种持久的基线-增量主动图框架，用于机器人在未知环境中自主构建并更新世界模型，通过分离稳定的结构基线与可变的动态对象事件，提升长期感知的可靠性与准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在长期服务机器人应用中存在定位误差累积、无法捕捉动态变化或缺乏可验证的3D几何证据等问题，难以支持持续的世界建模需求。

Method: 提出PBD-AG框架，将机器人验证的稳定结构作为基线，动态对象变化作为增量进行建模；通过机载探索自主初始化结构基线，并利用几何可见性门控机制减少遮挡下的误删；采用图条件策略选择检测视角，平衡覆盖性、成本与风险。

Result: 在多个环境的仿真实验中，PBD-AG在粗粒度固定装置的F1分数上优于能力匹配的对照方法，并表现出更强的身份连续性和事件回忆能力；实物机器人演示验证了其与机载传感器集成的可行性。

Conclusion: PBD-AG通过解耦稳定结构与动态对象，实现了可靠、可追溯的持久世界建模，为长时程服务机器人提供了有效的感知基础。
